- **paper-doc**: Generate documentation, specs, reports, architecture explainers, RFCs, briefs, and summaries as polished paper-and-ink HTML documents. ([Better-Social-Research/Urban-Research-Auto](https://github.com/Better-Social-Research/Urban-Research-Auto/tree/HEAD/.codex/skills/paper-lexicon-augmentor) · ★1)

- **paper-library**: 通过 paper-agent 管理 Frowang 远程论文库：上传 PDF、发现和查看论文、获取全文或报告、管理标签、笔记和 Collection。用户说“帮我查论文”“上传这篇 paper”“给论文打标签”“下载论文原文”时触发。 ([frowang-ai/paper-agent-skills](https://github.com/frowang-ai/paper-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/paper-library) · ★3)
- **paper-library**: Make a directory of research PDFs useful to an agent brainstorming with a user - build a grep-able catalog plus Markdown transcriptions, then query them. Use when pointed at a folder of papers, asked to index/catalog a paper collection, or asked which paper covers a topic. Triggers on "index these papers", "what paper covers X", "search the literature folder", or an AGENTS.md beside a pile of PDFs. ([Evidlo/dotfiles](https://github.com/Evidlo/dotfiles/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/paper-library))
- **paper-library**: Keep a local library of research papers as parsed markdown with one comparable note per paper and a generated topic index, and find research gaps in it on demand. Use when the user wants to add, import, re-fetch, summarise, sort or re-index papers in a local collection, asks "what's in my library on X", wants to build a paper database for a research area, or wants to check an idea for novelty, find gaps, or brainstorm directions against the papers they keep. Also when a project has a papers/ folder with BIBLIOGRAPHY.md or LIBRARY.md. ([lun-ai/skills](https://github.com/lun-ai/skills/tree/HEAD/paper-library))
- **paper-library**: Use when the user has a local folder of papers and wants to find something in it, ask a question of it, or organize it for later reading: keep one index file per directory, navigate by index instead of opening files, extract PDF text to sidecar files, and read only the located pages. Not for searching online (paper-search), reading one paper closely (qi-read-paper), or comparing papers (paper-compare). ([nerdneilsfield/skills](https://github.com/nerdneilsfield/skills/tree/HEAD/research/paper-library))
- **paper-library**: Use to manage/control the paper library created by the paper-quiz skill — browse or list papers, recap (re-show) a saved quiz, organize papers into projects, and add / move / delete / rename papers or projects. Also use to re-sync the catalog when the folders were edited by hand. This is the management counterpart to paper-quiz (which generates and grades the quizzes). Trigger on requests like "list my papers", "show my library", "show me the X quiz again", "move X to project Y", "add/delete this paper", "re-sync my library". ([Yuxiang-Chem/paper-quiz](https://github.com/Yuxiang-Chem/paper-quiz/tree/HEAD/paper-library))
- **paper-library-ingest**: Orchestrate complete paper-library ingestion for an ordered list of paper titles, identifiers, links, or PDF files, including acquisition, extraction, formula and Markdown cleanup, Chinese translation, notes, supplements, and index updates. Use for the full workflow; do not use for a single downstream transformation. ([QiuHong-1202/ResearchWorkbench](https://github.com/QiuHong-1202/ResearchWorkbench/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/paper-library-ingest) · ★1)

- **paper-lint**: Health-check and auto-repair the Obsidian wiki. Trigger when the user asks to lint, audit, health-check, or scan the wiki for quality issues. Automatically fixes repairable issues and writes a detailed report with diffs to the LintLog/ directory. - **paper-list-outreach**: Boost a paper's visibility/citations by adding it to curated "awesome list" / paper-list GitHub repos. Given a paper (arXiv link or metadata) and a target number N of relevant repos, discovers relevant repos across stated AND latent topics, skips repos that already list the paper, edits each repo locally in its exact format, and packages per-repo branches + patches + personalized PR messages + a ranked summary for the user to submit. Use when the user wants to promote a paper, get it into awesome lists, increase citations, or do paper outreach. - **paper-lit-search**: Multi-source literature search — query arXiv + Semantic Scholar + the local `resources/papers/` library for a topic, dedupe across sources, rank by relevance + recency + citations + project alignment, and save a search artifact at `resources/papers/searches/{YYYY-MM-DD}-{slug}.md`. Top hits worth deeper study chain into `paper-read`. Triggers on "search arxiv", "find papers about X", "lit search", "related work", "survey this topic", "文献搜索". Complements the existing `paper-read / paper-compare / paper-index` trio by covering the *discovery* step before any paper is read.
